========== OUTPUT [patient] ==========
I still can’t say it’s more than 5%, because I haven’t been checking my weight like that. I know I keep circling back to the scrubs, but that’s what I notice at work—they’re hanging loose, and I’m pulling them up more.

And eating… it’s just gotten off. Some days I’m running into a shift on coffee and maybe a granola bar, and then suddenly it’s hours later. So, yes, I’ve lost some weight without trying, I think, but I can’t honestly confirm the 5% number.
